\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
External potential and QMC/\+MM interface for quantum Monte Carlo calculations. 

This file provides a comprehensive interface for handling external potentials in quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) calculations, particularly for QMC/molecular mechanics (MM) hybrid approaches. It includes routines for reading and interpolating 3D potential grids (cube files), computing van der Waals interactions, managing link atoms, and handling molecular mechanics restraints.

\begin{DoxyAuthor}{Author}
E. Coccia and L. Guidoni (13/1/11), Turbo\+RVB group 
\end{DoxyAuthor}
\begin{DoxyDate}{Date}
2022
\end{DoxyDate}
\hypertarget{extv_8f90_Usage}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{Usage}{Usage}}\label{extv_8f90_Usage}
The module supports various external potential types\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item 3D potential grids from cube files
\item van der Waals interactions between QM and MM regions
\item Link atom management for QM/\+MM boundaries
\item Molecular mechanics restraints (bonds, angles, dihedrals)
\end{DoxyItemize}\hypertarget{extv_8f90_Examples}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{Examples}{Examples}}\label{extv_8f90_Examples}

subroutine compute\+\_\+dihed (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(3), intent(in)}]{ai}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(3), intent(in)}]{aj}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(3), intent(in)}]{ak}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(3), intent(in)}]{al}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, intent(out)}]{phi}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, intent(out)}]{cosphi}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(3), intent(out)}]{rij}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(3), intent(out)}]{rkj}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(3), intent(out)}]{rkl}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, intent(out)}]{rmj2}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, intent(out)}]{rnk2}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(3), intent(out)}]{rnk}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(3), intent(out)}]{rmj}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Calculate dihedral angle and related vectors for four atoms. 

This subroutine computes the dihedral angle phi and cosine of phi for four atoms (I-\/\+J-\/\+K-\/L) along with various intermediate vectors needed for dihedral potential calculations. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em ai} & Coordinates of first atom (I) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em aj} & Coordinates of second atom (J) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em ak} & Coordinates of third atom (K) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em al} & Coordinates of fourth atom (L) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em phi} & Dihedral angle in radians \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em cosphi} & Cosine of the dihedral angle \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em rij} & Vector from atom I to J \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em rkj} & Vector from atom K to J \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em rkl} & Vector from atom K to L \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em rmj2} & Square of cross product magnitude \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em rnk2} & Square of cross product magnitude \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em rnk} & Cross product vector \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em rmj} & Cross product vector \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Based on Gromos manual (II-\/21) implementation 
\end{DoxyNote}

subroutine deallocate\+\_\+extpot}



Deallocate memory used by external potential arrays. 

This subroutine deallocates all arrays associated with the external potential, including B-\/spline coefficients, knot sequences, potential grid, coordinate arrays, and atomic information.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Should be called at the end of calculations to free memory. All MPI processes should call this routine.
\end{DoxyNote}

\Hypertarget{extv_8f90_a295282d5460b7e4062d775d20483bcfd}\index{extv.f90@{extv.f90}!deallocate\_vdw@{deallocate\_vdw}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{deallocate\_vdw()}{deallocate\_vdw()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_a295282d5460b7e4062d775d20483bcfd} 
subroutine deallocate\+\_\+vdw}



Deallocate memory used by van der Waals arrays. 

This subroutine deallocates all arrays associated with van der Waals interactions, including coordinates, interaction parameters (C12, C6), atom type mappings, and special 1-\/4 interaction parameters if link atoms are used.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Should be called at the end of calculations to free memory. Conditionally deallocates cs12 and cs6 arrays if link\+\_\+atom is true.
\end{DoxyNote}

\Hypertarget{extv_8f90_af05a102ef110e46752357a04ca4c9836}\index{extv.f90@{extv.f90}!define\_link@{define\_link}}
\index{define\_link@{define\_link}!extv.f90@{extv.f90}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{define\_link()}{define\_link()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_af05a102ef110e46752357a04ca4c9836} 
subroutine define\+\_\+link}



Define logical arrays and indices for capping atoms in QM/\+MM framework. 

This subroutine creates logical arrays to identify capping atoms and sets up index arrays for capping atoms, QM-\/link atoms, and MM-\/link atoms. It maps the Gromos indices to the internal Turbo\+RVB/\+CPMD indices for efficient access during QM/\+MM calculations. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Creates log\+\_\+cap array to identify which atoms are capping atoms 

Sets up cap, qm, and prt arrays for direct index access 
\end{DoxyNote}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_af2254c3d1d1d1b3ff8ae730474f4a197} 
subroutine evaluate (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer}]{nxvec}{, }\item[{integer}]{nyvec}{, }\item[{integer}]{nzvec}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(nxvec)}]{xvec}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(nyvec)}]{yvec}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(nzvec)}]{zvec}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(nxvec, nyvec, nzvec)}]{value}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Evaluate the interpolated 3D potential at given points. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nxvec} & Number of x-\/coordinates \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nyvec} & Number of y-\/coordinates \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nzvec} & Number of z-\/coordinates \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em xvec} & Array of x-\/coordinates \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em yvec} & Array of y-\/coordinates \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em zvec} & Array of z-\/coordinates \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em value} & Array of interpolated potential values\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
This subroutine evaluates the 3D B-\/spline interpolated potential at the specified grid of points. It uses the B-\/spline coefficients computed in the interpolate routine to provide smooth potential values and derivatives.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
The interpolation provides smooth derivatives for force calculations in QMC/\+MM simulations.
\end{DoxyNote}

\Hypertarget{extv_8f90_a35324799cb04fd116b8375065de178d1}\index{extv.f90@{extv.f90}!exclusion\_list@{exclusion\_list}}
\index{exclusion\_list@{exclusion\_list}!extv.f90@{extv.f90}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{exclusion\_list()}{exclusion\_list()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_a35324799cb04fd116b8375065de178d1} 
subroutine exclusion\+\_\+list}



Read exclusion lists for van der Waals interactions from Gromos files. 

This subroutine reads exclusion lists from exclusion.\+dat and 14.\+dat files to determine which atom pairs should be excluded from van der Waals interactions. It handles both first/second neighbor exclusions and 1-\/4 neighbor interactions with different scaling factors. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Reads exclusion.\+dat for first and second neighbor exclusions 

Reads 14.\+dat for 1-\/4 neighbor interactions 

Only processes atoms that belong to the neighbor list (elist function) 
\end{DoxyNote}

\Hypertarget{extv_8f90_a0967ac914faf400d4364b2e47c04cda8}\index{extv.f90@{extv.f90}!extpot\_ene@{extpot\_ene}}
\index{extpot\_ene@{extpot\_ene}!extv.f90@{extv.f90}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{extpot\_ene()}{extpot\_ene()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_a0967ac914faf400d4364b2e47c04cda8} 
subroutine extpot\+\_\+ene (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(3, nelec)}]{coord}{, }\item[{integer}]{nelec}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(nelec)}]{ext\+\_\+ene}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8}]{sum\+\_\+ext}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Evaluate external potential energy for electrons in QMC calculations. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em coord} & Electron coordinates (3, nelec) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nelec} & Number of electrons \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em ext\+\_\+ene} & External potential energy for each electron \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em sum\+\_\+ext} & Total external potential energy\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
This subroutine evaluates the external potential energy for each electron by interpolating the 3D potential grid at their positions. It checks if electrons are within the potential box boundaries and accumulates statistical data for energy averaging in QMC calculations.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Electrons outside the potential box are counted but assigned zero energy. The routine updates statistical accumulators for energy averaging.
\end{DoxyNote}

\Hypertarget{extv_8f90_ad185555b69aec68524956a2a7ce7f349}\index{extv.f90@{extv.f90}!extpot\_final@{extpot\_final}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{extpot\_final()}{extpot\_final()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_ad185555b69aec68524956a2a7ce7f349} 
subroutine extpot\+\_\+final (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer, intent(in)}]{nelec}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Print final statistics for external potential calculations. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nelec} & Number of electrons\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
This subroutine computes and prints final statistical results for the external potential calculations, including the average energy, error estimates, and the rate of electrons found outside the potential box.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Uses MPI-\/averaged statistics (t\+\_\+\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} variables) for parallel runs. The error is computed as the standard deviation of the mean.
\end{DoxyNote}
\hypertarget{extv_8f90_Examples}{}\doxysubsection{\texorpdfstring{Examples}{Examples}}\label{extv_8f90_Examples}

\Hypertarget{extv_8f90_aabf311a099d8e3caa708059ca73eb25b}\index{extv.f90@{extv.f90}!extpot\_read@{extpot\_read}}
\index{extpot\_read@{extpot\_read}!extv.f90@{extv.f90}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{extpot\_read()}{extpot\_read()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_aabf311a099d8e3caa708059ca73eb25b} 
subroutine extpot\+\_\+read}



Read external potential data from a cube file and set up interpolation. 

This subroutine reads a 3D potential grid from a Gaussian cube file format and prepares it for interpolation. The cube file contains atomic positions, grid dimensions, and potential values on a regular 3D grid. The routine allocates memory for the potential array and coordinate grids, then calls the interpolation routine to set up spline coefficients for smooth evaluation.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
The cube file must be named \textquotesingle{}potential.\+cube\textquotesingle{} and follow the standard Gaussian cube file format with atomic positions and 3D potential grid.

This routine initializes statistical accumulators for energy averaging in QMC calculations.
\end{DoxyNote}

\Hypertarget{extv_8f90_a37f734d389f513a4aedbdf996875e00f}\index{extv.f90@{extv.f90}!interpolate@{interpolate}}
\index{interpolate@{interpolate}!extv.f90@{extv.f90}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{interpolate()}{interpolate()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_a37f734d389f513a4aedbdf996875e00f} 
subroutine interpolate}



Set up 3D spline interpolation for the external potential. 

This subroutine prepares the interpolation parameters for the 3D potential grid using B-\/splines of order 5 in all three dimensions. It generates knot sequences and computes the B-\/spline coefficients for smooth evaluation of the potential at arbitrary points within the grid domain.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Uses B-\/spline interpolation with order 5 for all dimensions. The interpolation provides smooth derivatives for force calculations.

Must be called after extpot\+\_\+read to set up the interpolation.
\end{DoxyNote}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ion\_ene()}{ion\_ene()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_ad0d7b18e9f03752ea9d9b924732a9dda} 
subroutine ion\+\_\+ene (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(3, nion)}]{rion}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(nion)}]{zeta}{, }\item[{integer}]{nion}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(nion)}]{ext\+\_\+ion}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8}]{sum\+\_\+ion}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Evaluate external potential energy for ions in QMC calculations. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em rion} & Ion coordinates (3, nion) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em zeta} & Ion charges (length nion) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nion} & Number of ions \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em ext\+\_\+ion} & External potential energy for each ion \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em sum\+\_\+ion} & Total external potential energy for ions\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
This subroutine evaluates the external potential energy for each ion by interpolating the 3D potential grid at their positions. The energy is weighted by the ion charge and accumulated for statistical analysis.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
The potential is evaluated at all ion positions without boundary checks. The energy is accumulated for averaging in QMC calculations.
\end{DoxyNote}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ion\_final()}{ion\_final()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_a2f2e9a87916e671f48c29e2e4e50bd10} 
subroutine ion\+\_\+final (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer, intent(in)}]{nion}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Print final statistics for ion external potential calculations. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nion} & Number of ions\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
This subroutine computes and prints final statistical results for the ion external potential calculations, including the average energy and error estimates. It also computes the total QMC/\+MM energy combining electronic and nuclear contributions.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Uses MPI-\/averaged statistics (t\+\_\+\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} variables) for parallel runs. The nuclear term is treated as a constant in wave function optimization. Error handling prevents NaN values in statistical calculations.
\end{DoxyNote}

\Hypertarget{extv_8f90_a2bfc87036924f7b9c1591ebbff4afac1}\index{extv.f90@{extv.f90}!link\_read@{link\_read}}
\index{link\_read@{link\_read}!extv.f90@{extv.f90}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{link\_read()}{link\_read()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_a2bfc87036924f7b9c1591ebbff4afac1} 
subroutine link\+\_\+read}



Read link atom configuration and parameters from link.\+dat file. 

This subroutine reads the link.\+dat file which contains the configuration for QM/\+MM link atoms including capping atoms, QM-\/link atoms, and MM-\/link atoms. It also reads angular, dihedral, and improper dihedral parameters for molecular mechanics restraints in the QM/\+MM framework. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
The link.\+dat file format includes\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Number of link atoms
\item Gromos indices for capping, QM-\/link, and MM-\/link atoms
\item Angular contribution parameters (K\+\_\+theta, cos(theta\+\_\+eq))
\item Proper dihedral parameters (K\+\_\+phi, multiplicity, cos(phi\+\_\+eq))
\item Improper dihedral parameters (K\+\_\+qhi, cos(qhi\+\_\+eq)) 
\end{DoxyItemize}
\end{DoxyNote}

\Hypertarget{extv_8f90_a01049841d03b91a92301b9efe1bfd993}\index{extv.f90@{extv.f90}!mm\_angle@{mm\_angle}}
\index{mm\_angle@{mm\_angle}!extv.f90@{extv.f90}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{mm\_angle()}{mm\_angle()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_a01049841d03b91a92301b9efe1bfd993} 
subroutine mm\+\_\+angle}



Calculate molecular mechanics bond angle contributions in QM/\+MM framework. 

This subroutine calculates the bond angle potential energy contribution for triplets involving QM-\/link and MM-\/link atoms. The potential follows the harmonic form\+: 1/2 \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} k\+\_\+theta \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} (cos(theta) -\/ cos(theta\+\_\+eq))\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}2 where theta is the angle between three atoms (I-\/\+J-\/K). \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Handles both QM and MM atoms in the angle calculations 

Accumulates statistics for bond angle potential energy 

Based on Gromos force field implementation (see Gromos manual II-\/17) 
\end{DoxyNote}

\Hypertarget{extv_8f90_a71bd355cd3f57033a691f88174c856fb}\index{extv.f90@{extv.f90}!mm\_dihed@{mm\_dihed}}
\index{mm\_dihed@{mm\_dihed}!extv.f90@{extv.f90}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{mm\_dihed()}{mm\_dihed()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_a71bd355cd3f57033a691f88174c856fb} 
subroutine mm\+\_\+dihed}



Calculate molecular mechanics proper dihedral contributions in QM/\+MM framework. 

This subroutine calculates the proper dihedral potential energy contribution for quadruplets involving QM-\/link and MM-\/link atoms. The potential follows the form\+: k\+\_\+phi \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} (1 + cos(phi\+\_\+eq) \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} cos(mult \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} phi)) where phi is the dihedral angle between four atoms (I-\/\+J-\/\+K-\/L). \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Handles both QM and MM atoms in the dihedral calculations 

Accumulates statistics for proper dihedral potential energy 

Based on Gromos force field implementation (see Gromos manual II-\/26) 
\end{DoxyNote}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{mm\_improper()}{mm\_improper()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_a2b2c1d363b80577d83c0c15c0827f196} 
subroutine mm\+\_\+improper}



Calculate molecular mechanics improper dihedral contributions in QM/\+MM framework. 

This subroutine calculates the improper dihedral potential energy contribution for quadruplets involving QM-\/link and MM-\/link atoms. The potential follows the harmonic form\+: 1/2 \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} k\+\_\+qhi \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} (phi -\/ phi\+\_\+eq)\texorpdfstring{$^\wedge$}{\string^}2 where phi is the improper dihedral angle between four atoms (I-\/\+J-\/\+K-\/L). \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Handles both QM and MM atoms in the improper dihedral calculations 

Accumulates statistics for improper dihedral potential energy 

Uses modulo function to handle periodicity in dihedral angles 
\end{DoxyNote}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_af608609a0b8e7dfbc2302be5e1fe329f} 
subroutine r\+\_\+angle}



Calculate classical bond angle restraints. 

This subroutine calculates harmonic bond angle restraints around the equilibrium angle. The potential follows the form\+: 1/2 \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} mm\+\_\+fact \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} k\+\_\+theta \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} (cos(theta) -\/ cos(theta\+\_\+eq))\texorpdfstring{$^\wedge$}{\string^}2 \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Accumulates statistics for bond angle restraint potential energy 
\end{DoxyNote}

\Hypertarget{extv_8f90_a44875c4507eb75301765971146a5c117}\index{extv.f90@{extv.f90}!r\_capping@{r\_capping}}
\index{r\_capping@{r\_capping}!extv.f90@{extv.f90}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{r\_capping()}{r\_capping()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_a44875c4507eb75301765971146a5c117} 
subroutine r\+\_\+capping}



Position capping atoms in QM/\+MM link atom framework. 

This subroutine positions capping atoms along the QM-\/link/\+MM-\/link bond according to the specified scaling factor alpha. The capping atom is positioned at a fraction alpha of the distance from QM-\/link to MM-\/link atom. This implementation uses a constant alpha value rather than the original distance-\/based approach. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
The capping atom is not an independent variable but is constrained to lie on the QM-\/link/\+MM-\/link bond vector. 

Based on the method described in Theor. Chem. Acc., vol. 100, 307 (1998) 
\end{DoxyNote}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_add2a2ee704fa2d1042dc4f786e7aeae3} 
subroutine r\+\_\+dihe}



Calculate classical proper dihedral restraints. 

This subroutine calculates harmonic proper dihedral restraints around the equilibrium dihedral angle. The potential follows the form\+: 1/2 \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} mm\+\_\+fact \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} k\+\_\+phi \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} (cos(phi) -\/ cos(phi\+\_\+eq))\texorpdfstring{$^\wedge$}{\string^}2 \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Uses compute\+\_\+dihed subroutine for dihedral angle calculation 

Accumulates statistics for proper dihedral restraint potential energy 
\end{DoxyNote}

\end{DoxyNote}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{restr\_read()}{restr\_read()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_ab51a6fe496924d2a1d39451675989caa} 
subroutine restr\+\_\+read}



Read classical restraints for restrained wave function and geometry optimizations. 

This subroutine reads classical molecular mechanics restraints from restr.\+dat file for use in QM/\+MM calculations with mm\+\_\+restr=.true. It reads bond, angle, dihedral, and improper dihedral restraints with their force constants and equilibrium values. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Reads multiplicative factor for restraint scaling 

Converts angle values from degrees to radians and to cosine values 

Allocates force arrays for each restraint type 
\end{DoxyNote}

\Hypertarget{extv_8f90_a1c08641040ff8b0c27bd930ecea9b03b}\index{extv.f90@{extv.f90}!vdw\_ene@{vdw\_ene}}
\index{vdw\_ene@{vdw\_ene}!extv.f90@{extv.f90}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{vdw\_ene()}{vdw\_ene()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_a1c08641040ff8b0c27bd930ecea9b03b} 
subroutine vdw\+\_\+ene (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(3, nion), intent(in)}]{rion}{, }\item[{integer, intent(in)}]{nion}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, intent(inout)}]{sum\+\_\+vdw}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Calculate van der Waals interaction energy between QM and MM atoms. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em rion} & Ion coordinates (3, nion) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nion} & Number of ions \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em sum\+\_\+vdw} & Total van der Waals energy\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
This subroutine computes the van der Waals interaction between QM and MM (classical) atoms using the Lennard-\/\+Jones formula\+: V\+\_\+\+QMC\+\_\+\+NN = \textbackslash{}sum\+\_\+\+QMC \textbackslash{}sum\+\_\+\+NN \mbox{[} C12\+\_\+\+QMC\+\_\+\+NN/(R\+\_\+\+QMC\+\_\+\+NN)\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}12 -\/ C6\+\_\+\+QMC\+\_\+\+NN/(R\+\_\+\+QMC\+\_\+\+NN)\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}6 \mbox{]}

The routine handles exclusion lists for link atoms and special 1-\/4 interactions when link atoms are present. It accumulates statistical data for energy averaging in QMC calculations.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Supports both simple QM/\+MM and link atom scenarios. Uses different parameters for 1-\/4 interactions when link atoms are present.
\end{DoxyNote}

\Hypertarget{extv_8f90_abdec9216df30b09196e063f9eb7d5bcb}\index{extv.f90@{extv.f90}!vdw\_final@{vdw\_final}}
\index{vdw\_final@{vdw\_final}!extv.f90@{extv.f90}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{vdw\_final()}{vdw\_final()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_abdec9216df30b09196e063f9eb7d5bcb} 
subroutine vdw\+\_\+final}



Print final statistics for van der Waals and MM restraint calculations. 

This subroutine computes and prints final statistical results for van der Waals interactions and molecular mechanics restraints (bonds, angles, dihedrals, impropers). It handles both simple QM/\+MM and link atom scenarios.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Uses MPI-\/averaged statistics (t\+\_\+\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} variables) for parallel runs. Error handling prevents NaN values in statistical calculations. Different output formats depending on whether link atoms or MM restraints are used.
\end{DoxyNote}

\Hypertarget{extv_8f90_aee845b2e8c1a963283929bb32465a1f0}\index{extv.f90@{extv.f90}!vdw\_read@{vdw\_read}}
\index{vdw\_read@{vdw\_read}!extv.f90@{extv.f90}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{vdw\_read()}{vdw\_read()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_aee845b2e8c1a963283929bb32465a1f0} 
subroutine vdw\+\_\+read}



Read van der Waals parameters and atom information from vdw.\+dat file. 

This subroutine reads van der Waals interaction parameters (C12, C6) and atom information from the vdw.\+dat file. It sets up the data structures needed for computing van der Waals interactions between QM and MM regions in QMC/\+MM calculations.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
The file must contain\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Number of Gromos vdW types and QM atoms
\item C12 and C6 parameters for each atom type pair
\item Atom information including coordinates and types
\item Special parameters for 1-\/4 interactions if link atoms are used
\end{DoxyItemize}

The number of QM atoms in the file must match nion. The routine allocates memory for vdW arrays and coordinates.
\end{DoxyNote}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{write\_traj()}{write\_traj()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{extv_8f90_a68bac1692da8467361d2a05c0704cb81} 
subroutine write\+\_\+traj (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer, intent(in)}]{nel}{, }\item[{integer, intent(in)}]{nelup}{, }\item[{double precision, dimension(3, nel), intent(in)}]{kel}{, }\item[{double precision, intent(in)}]{eloc}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Write electron trajectory data for visualization. 

This subroutine writes electron positions to trajectory file for visualization purposes. It converts atomic units to Angstroms and distinguishes between up and down electrons with \textquotesingle{}N\textquotesingle{} and \textquotesingle{}O\textquotesingle{} labels respectively. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nel} & Total number of electrons \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nelup} & Number of up-\/spin electrons \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em kel} & Electron coordinates (3, nel) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em eloc} & Local energy value \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Only called when rank.\+eq.\+0 (first walker) 

Writes to unit 671 in XYZ format 

Converts coordinates from atomic units to Angstroms 
\end{DoxyNote}
